- `truncateSummary` — `function truncateSummary: (text: string, maxLength: number) => string`
- `truncateWithEllipsis` — `function truncateWithEllipsis: (text: string, maxLen: number) => string`
- `UsageRoute` — `type UsageRoute = z.infer<typeof UsageRouteSchema>; ≡ "api-key" | "chatgpt-subscription" | "glm-coding-plan-subscription" | "kimi-code-subscription" | "xai-subscription"`
- `UserFollowUpSupport` — `type UserFollowUpSupport = z.infer<typeof UserFollowUpSupportSchema>; ≡ "nativeInteractive" | "terminalBacked" | "unsupported"`
- `WorkspaceFileLocation` — `type WorkspaceFileLocation = z.infer<typeof WorkspaceFileLocationSchema>; ≡ { absolutePath: string; kind: "workspace"; relativePath: string; }`
- `WorkspaceStateKey` — `enum WorkspaceStateKey { WORKSPACE_AGENTS = 'texra.workspaceAgents', CUSTOM_TEAMS = 'texra.customTeams', HIDDEN_CUSTOM_AGENTS = 'texra.hiddenCustomAgents', DISABLED_SKILLS = 'texra.skills.disabled', DISABLED_SKILL_SOURCES = 'texra.skills.disabledSources', GIT_MARK_COMMITS = 'texra.git.markCommits', GIT_AUTHOR_NAME = 'texra.git.authorName', GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL = 'texra.git.authorEmail', GIT_WORKTREE_SUPPORT = 'texra.git.worktreeSupport', TOOL_PATH_PROTECTION_ENABLED = 'texra.tools.restrictPathsToWorkingDirectory' }`
